# avatar cleanup report

Phase avatar recheck during 10; base `ec2b646a3ad64b68d0d50329996b5fa3ff3c2ace`.

Integrated the 13 additional main commits through ec2b646a, preserving data-backed wardrobe expansion and repairing defects reproduced on that exact main revision.

| Original file | Decision | Responsibility and usage evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `__init__.py` | KEEP | Headless AVATAR state, wardrobe, presentation, and authoring subsystem. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |
| `authoring.py` | KEEP | Latest content-rating/exposure fields are passed into authored designs; earlier retirement of redundant type cache remains. |
| `clothing_action.py` | KEEP | Authoritative state-changing service for AVATAR clothing actions. Imported by `application/bootstrap.py`. |
| `clothing_intent.py` | KEEP | Natural-language clothing intent parsing for AVATAR wardrobe actions. Imported by `avatar/wardrobe_autonomy.py`, `avatar/clothing_action.py`. |
| `fit.py` | KEEP | Body-region mappings and validated fit anchors for wardrobe blueprints. Imported by `avatar/wardrobe_catalog.py`. |
| `matrix.py` | KEEP | AVATAR contribution to the message matrix. Imported by `cognition/matrix/defaults.py`. |
| `presentation.py` | KEEP | Headless authoritative AVATAR presentation state. | `presentation_store.py` | KEEP | Durable SQLite persistence for headless AVATAR presentation state. Imported by `avatar/presentation_runtime.py`, `avatar/presentation_routine.py`, `avatar/authoring.py`, `application/bootstrap.py`. |

| `wardrobe_design.py` | SPLIT | Preserved latest content-rating/exposure validation and positional GarmentDesign compatibility. Expanded type definitions live in wardrobe_types.py. |
| `wardrobe_matrix.py` | KEEP | Latest rating/exposure cell projections retained; canonical presentation runtime consumes the matrix. |
| `wardrobe_planner.py` | KEEP | Fixed double counting of the same grounded emotion across garment profiles and outfit tags; combined contribution obeys the existing three-point cap. |

Latest main expanded garment types, added STANDARD/LEWD/EXPLICIT metadata and exposure validation, preserved positional design compatibility, and packaged seven JSON garment catalogs. The two merge conflicts were resolved by retaining new main semantics in the earlier canonical type/prebuild owners, with AST comparisons of every moved definition and exact byte comparisons of all JSON files. Added wardrobe_loader.py for the distinct JSON schema/typed profile decoding responsibility; catalog injects its existing blueprint factory, avoiding a reverse dependency. Existing authoring and matrix feature additions remain.

All nine initial avatar gate failures were reproduced in a source/test snapshot of origin/main (9 failed, 230 passed). Fixed stale catalog assumptions: underlayer count scoped to that category, family lookup uses garment_type(design.garment_type), profile assertions match new authoritative JSON, available and referenced profile roles distinguished. The no-replacement negative test now supplies a bounded preset-only catalog; added expanded-inventory swap regression checks public coverage and persisted authority. Planner was counting one emotion in item profiles plus outfit tags; it now shares the existing three-point budget without changing daypart/engineering assertions. No private exposure/coverage assertion was removed.

JSON profile inventory:
- `bottoms.json`: 20 records; profile templates athletic, casual, cool_weather, denim, hot_weather, lounge, private_explicit, private_lewd, smart, technical; referenced templates athletic, denim, hot_weather, lounge, private_explicit, private_lewd, smart, technical.
- `footwear.json`: 20 records; profile templates athletic, casual, cold_weather, lounge, outdoor, smart, summer, wet_weather, work; referenced templates athletic, casual, cold_weather, lounge, outdoor, smart, summer, wet_weather, work.
- `one_pieces.json`: 20 records; profile templates athletic, casual, cold_weather, private_explicit, private_lewd, smart, summer, technical; referenced templates athletic, casual, cold_weather, private_explicit, private_lewd, smart, summer, technical.
- `outerwear.json`: 20 records; profile templates casual, cold_weather, cool_weather, lounge, rain, smart, technical, wind; referenced templates casual, cold_weather, cool_weather, lounge, rain, smart, technical, wind.
- `tops.json`: 20 records; profile templates athletic, casual, cold_weather, cool_weather, hot_weather, lounge, private_explicit, private_lewd, smart, technical; referenced templates athletic, casual, cold_weather, cool_weather, hot_weather, lounge, private_explicit, private_lewd, smart, technical.
- `underlayers_lower.json`: 24 records; profile templates athletic, compression, cooling, private_explicit, private_lewd, sleep, soft_everyday, thermal; referenced templates athletic, compression, cooling, private_explicit, private_lewd, sleep, soft_everyday, thermal.
- `underlayers_upper.json`: 24 records; profile templates cooling, private_explicit, private_lewd, sleep, soft_everyday, sport, structured_support, thermal; referenced templates cooling, private_explicit, private_lewd, sleep, soft_everyday, sport, structured_support, thermal.

New canonical files: wardrobe_types.py (expanded type vocabulary), wardrobe_prebuild.py (new main contracts/metadata), wardrobe_loader.py (validated JSON decoder). Their class/type contracts preserve latest main semantics.

## Validation and remaining scope

Compile passed. Avatar/interaction-avatar/semantic embodiment/composition/Matrix/package ownership gate: 365 passed (7.50s). Supported isolated wheel build succeeded; all seven packaged JSON resources matched source bytes exactly. Initial --no-build-isolation build lacked setuptools; default PEP 517 build isolation resolved the build prerequisite. Final full-suite verification follows the remaining Phase 10 folders. Snapshot comparison did not create another Git checkout/worktree or alter source main.

JSON files own the new catalog/profile metadata; wardrobe_types owns garment type constraints; wardrobe_design owns immutable design/content validation; wardrobe_prebuild owns blueprint metadata; presentation authority/store remain canonical worn state. Metadata never proves a renderer asset or visible clothing. No JSON/resource or protected identity/constitution asset bytes were changed.

Checkpoint: `git log -1 --format=%H -- docs/development/avatar-latest-wardrobe-merge-report.md`.
